We introduce multimodal optimization modeling, a benchmark setting in which models must construct both a mathematical formulation and executable solver code from a text-and-visual problem specification. To evaluate this setting, we develop a solver-grounded framework that generates structured optimization instances, verifies each with an exact solver, and builds both the model-facing inputs and hidden reference files from the same verified source. We instantiate the framework as MM-OptBench, a benchmark of 780 solver-verified instances spanning 6 optimization families, 26 subcategories, and 3 structural difficulty levels. We evaluate 9 multimodal large language models (MLLMs), including 6 frontier general-purpose models and 3 math-specialized models, with aggregate, family-level, difficulty-level, and failure-mode analyses. The results show that the task remains far from solved: the best two models reach 52.1% and 51.3% pass@1, while on average across the six general-purpose MLLMs, pass@1 is 43.4% on easy instances and 15.9% on hard instances. All three math-specialized MLLMs solve 0/780 instances. Failure attribution shows that errors arise both when extracting instance data from text and visuals and when turning extracted data into solver-correct formulations and code. MM-OptBench provides a testbed for solver-grounded, decision-oriented multimodal intelligence.

</details>


### [146] [Goal-Oriented Reasoning for RAG-based Memory in Conversational Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12213)
*Jiazhou Liang,Armin Toroghi,Yifan Simon Liu,Faeze Moradi Kalarde,Liam Gallagher,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Goal-Mem 是一种面向目标的推理框架，用于基于 RAG 的智能体记忆系统。它通过将用户话语作为目标进行显式反向链式分解，生成原子性子目标并针对性检索记忆，以解决长对话中多跳推理和隐式推断的难题。在自然语言逻辑形式化下，该方法在两个数据集上优于九个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 RAG 的记忆方法通常仅基于语义相似度检索原始用户话语，缺乏对缺失中间事实的显式推理，常返回不相关或不足以支撑推理的证据，导致长对话中智能体行为不连贯。

Method: Goal-Mem 提出面向目标的推理框架：将用户话语作为目标，通过显式反向链分解为原子性子目标，针对每个子目标进行记忆检索；当中间目标无法解决时迭代识别需要检索的记忆信息；整个过程使用自然语言逻辑（结合 FOL 的可验证性与自然语言的表达力）进行形式化。

Result: 在两个数据集上，与九个强基线记忆方法对比，Goal-Mem 持续提升性能，特别是在需要多跳推理和隐式推断的任务上表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: Goal-Mem 通过目标分解与反向链推理，有效增强了 RAG 智能体在长对话中对复杂查询的记忆检索与推理能力，验证了显式推理在记忆管理中的重要性。

Abstract: LLM-based conversational AI agents struggle to maintain coherent behavior over long horizons due to limited context. While RAG-based approaches are increasingly adopted to overcome this limitation by storing interactions in external memory modules and performing retrieval from them, their effectiveness in answering challenging questions (e.g., multi-hop, commonsense) ultimately depends on the agent's ability to reason over the retrieved information. However, existing methods typically retrieve memory based on semantic similarity to the raw user utterance, which lacks explicit reasoning about missing intermediate facts and often returns evidence that is irrelevant or insufficient for grounded reasoning. In this work, we introduce Goal-Mem, a goal-oriented reasoning framework for RAG-based agentic memory that performs explicit backward chaining from the user's utterance as a goal. Rather than progressively expanding from retrieved context, Goal-Mem decomposes each goal into atomic subgoals, performs targeted memory retrieval to satisfy each subgoal, and iteratively identifies what information from memory should be retrieved when intermediate goals cannot be resolved. We formalize this process in Natural Language Logic, a logical system that combines the verifiability of reasoning provided by FOL with the expressivity of natural language. Through extensive experiments on two datasets and comparing to nine strong memory baselines, we show that Goal-Mem consistently improves performance, particularly on tasks requiring multi-hop reasoning and implicit inference.

</details>


### [147] [Why Conclusions Diverge from the Same Observations: Formalizing World-Model Non-Identifiability via an Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12255)
*Toru Takahashi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，人们在共享相同数据和观测时得出不同结论，并非源于认知缺陷或恶意，而是推理与学习中固有的非可识别性。论文将其分为θ级（相同世界模型下因推理设置不同导致结论分歧）和W级（推理设置的反复使用导致世界模型本身发生偏离）两个层面，并引入推理轮廓θ=(R,E,S,D)来解释这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在重新解释人们在共享相同信息时产生分歧的现象，驳斥将分歧归因于对方认知缺陷或非理性的常见偏见，主张分歧是推理与学习中固有的非可识别性问题。

Method: 通过形式化定义推理轮廓θ=(R,E,S,D)（参考、探索、稳定、视野），构建了两层非可识别性框架：θ级（同世界模型下不同推理设置导致结论分歧）和W级（推理设置反复使用导致世界模型本身偏离）。进一步论证分歧会投影到少数基（抽象vs具体、外部化、秩序vs自由）上，并联系深度表征学习中的表征层次、潜状态估计、正则化-探索权衡等概念。

Result: 建立了分歧的非可识别性解释框架，阐明了即使在同一观测和世界模型下，推理设置的差异也会导致结论分裂；并解释了分歧为何倾向于投影到少数几个基本维度，这源于学习系统的计算约束、观测约束和协调约束。

Conclusion: 分歧应被理解为推理过程中固有的非可识别性现象，而非对方的认知缺陷；该框架有助于在AI监管等争议性议题中达成更理性的理解。

Abstract: When people share the same documents and observations yet reach different conclusions, the disagreement often shifts into a judgment that the other party is cognitively defective, irrational, or acting in bad faith. This paper argues that such divergence is better described as a form of non-identifiability inherent in inference and learning, rather than as a defect of the other party. We organize the phenomenon into two levels: (i) $θ$-level non-identifiability, where conclusions diverge under the same world model $W$ because inference settings differ; and (ii) $W$-level non-identifiability, where repeated use of an inference setting $θ$ biases data exposure and update rules, causing the learned world model $W$ itself to diverge. We introduce an inference profile $θ= (R, E, S, D)$, consisting of Reference, Exploration, Stabilization, and Horizon, and show how outputs can split even for the same observation $o$ and the same $W$. We further explain why disagreements tend to project onto a small number of bases -- abstract versus concrete, externalizability, and order versus freedom -- as a consequence of general constraints on learning systems: computational, observational, and coordination constraints. Finally, we relate the framework to deep representation learning, including representation hierarchy, latent-state estimation, and regularization-exploration trade-offs, and illustrate the framework through a case study on AI regulation debates.

</details>


### [148] [Missingness-MDPs: Bridging the Theory of Missing Data and POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12262)
*Joshua Wendland,Markel Zubia,Roman Andriushchenko,Maris F. L. Galesloot,Milan Ceska,Henrik von Kleist,Thiago D. Simao,Maximilian Weininger,Nils Jansen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出缺失MDP（miss-MDP）框架，将缺失数据理论融入部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，在未知缺失函数下通过学习不同缺失类型（MCAR/MAR/MNAR）的结构性质，实现PAC可学习的高概率ε-最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在POMDP中，状态特征可能因传感器故障、通信限制等原因而缺失（未观测），传统POMDP难以处理缺失数据问题且缺失函数未知时无法直接应用；需要一种框架将缺失数据理论与序列决策结合，在可学习性保证下规划近似最优策略。

Method: 1) 形式化定义miss-MDP，其观测函数为缺失函数，建模各状态特征在每个时间步的缺失概率；2) 根据三种缺失类型（MCAR、MAR、MNAR）的结构性质，设计PAC学习算法分别估计缺失函数；3) 基于学习得到的近似缺失函数构建完整的近似miss-MDP，使用现成规划方法求解；4) 理论证明所得策略在真实miss-MDP中以高概率达到ε-最优。

Result: 理论证明了PAC学习算法的收敛性和策略的ε-最优性保证；实验结果表明该方法优于两种无模型的POMDP基线方法。

Conclusion: miss-MDP框架有效弥合了缺失数据理论与序列决策规划之间的鸿沟，利用缺失类型结构性质实现了在未知缺失函数下的可学习性和策略最优性保证，为实际中的不完整观测决策问题提供了理论基础和可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce missingness-MDPs (miss-MDPs), a novel subclass of partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) that incorporates the theory of missing data. A miss-MDP is a POMDP whose observation function is a missingness function, specifying the probability that individual state features are missing (i.e., unobserved) at a time step. The literature distinguishes three canonical missingness types: missing (1) completely at random (MCAR), (2) at random (MAR), and (3) not at random (MNAR). Our planning problem is to compute near-optimal policies for a miss-MDP with an unknown missingness function, given a dataset of action-observation trajectories. Achieving such optimality guarantees for policies requires learning the missingness function from data, which is infeasible for general POMDPs. To overcome this challenge, we exploit the structural properties of different missingness types to derive probably approximately correct (PAC) algorithms for learning the missingness function. These algorithms yield an approximate but fully specified miss-MDP that we solve using off-the-shelf planning methods. We prove that, with high probability, the resulting policies are epsilon-optimal in the true miss-MDP. Empirical results confirm the theory and demonstrate superior performance of our approach over two model-free POMDP methods.

</details>


### [149] [How Useful Is Cross-Domain Generalization for Training LLM Monitors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12265)
*Sam Martin,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多任务分类训练（带提示）对域外泛化的影响，发现其能部分泛化到相邻领域，但在特定边缘情况（如提示完全改变但数据域不变）下会失效。混合通用指令跟随训练可缓解该问题。令人惊讶的是，无推理分类训练可泛化到有推理分类和摘要任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示语言模型作为分类器在有限训练数据场景下有效，但缺乏微调带来的鲁棒性和性能优势。作者希望探究多任务分类训练是否能提升新领域新提示下的分类性能。

Method: 在多个分类任务上（每个任务有其对应提示）进行训练，评估其在未见过的域外分类任务上的表现；识别模型无法跟随提示的边界情况；将分类训练与通用指令跟随训练混合，观察效果。

Result: 多任务分类训练可部分泛化到相邻领域；当分类提示完全改变而数据域不变时，模型无法跟随提示；混合通用指令跟随训练可保持分类训练优势并缓解泛化失败；无推理分类训练可泛化到有推理分类和摘要任务。

Conclusion: 多任务分类训练具有一定的域外泛化能力但存在局限性，混合通用指令跟随训练是有效的缓解策略，且无推理分类训练可能对构建其他分类器和监控系统具有工具性价值。

Abstract: Using prompted language models as classifiers enables classification in domains with limited training data, but misses some of the robustness and performance benefits that fine-tuning can bring. We study whether training on multiple classification tasks, each with its own prompt, improves performance on new domains with new classification prompts. We show that such training partially generalizes to adjacent domains, improving classification performance on tasks that are unseen during training. However, we identify specific edge cases where the fine-tuned models fail to follow prompts, such as when the classification prompt changes completely while the data domain remains the same as during training. We show that classification training can be mixed with general instruction following training, and that (when done well) such training keeps the benefits of classification training and mitigates its generalization failures. Surprisingly, we see that this no-thinking supervised classification training can generalize to with-thinking classification and summarization, suggesting that no-thinking classification training might be instrumentally useful in building other kinds of classifiers and monitoring systems.

</details>


### [150] [NARA: Anchor-Conditioned Relation-Aware Contextualization of Heterogeneous Geoentities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12276)
*Jina Kim,Gengchen Mai,Lingyi Zhao,Khurram Shafique,Yao-Yi Chiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NARA框架，通过联合建模语义、几何和空间关系（包括度量邻近和拓扑关系），为矢量地理实体学习上下文相关的表示，在建筑功能分类、交通速度预测和下一兴趣点推荐任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矢量地理数据表示学习方法碎片化，通常局限于特定几何类型或部分空间关系（仅度量邻近），无法统一捕获异构地理实体之间的完整空间上下文。

Method: 提出NARA，一种基于神经锚点条件的自监督关系感知表示学习框架，联合建模语义、几何和空间关系（度量邻近+拓扑关系），支持点、折线、多边形等多种几何类型的异构地理实体。

Result: 在建筑功能分类、交通速度预测和下一兴趣点推荐三个任务上均持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 统一的关系建模对于矢量地理数据表示学习具有显著优势，超越了仅依赖度量邻近的方法。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models have primarily focused on raster data such as satellite imagery, where self-supervised learning has been widely studied. Vector geospatial data instead represent the world as discrete geoentities with explicit geometry, semantics, and structured spatial relations, including metric proximity and topological relationships. These relations jointly determine how entities interact within space, yet existing representation learning methods remain fragmented, often restricted to specific geometry types or partial spatial relations, limiting their ability to capture unified spatial context across heterogeneous geoentities. We propose NARA (Neural Anchor-conditioned Relation-Aware representation learning), a self-supervised framework for vector geoentities. NARA learns context-dependent representations by jointly modeling semantics, geometry, and spatial relations within a unified framework and captures relational spatial structure beyond proximity alone, enabling rich contextualized representations across heterogeneous geoentities of points, polylines, and polygons. Evaluation on building function classification, traffic speed prediction, and next point-of-interest recommendation shows consistent improvements over prior methods, highlighting the benefit of unified relational modeling for vector geospatial data.

</details>


### [151] [Executable Agentic Memory for GUI Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12294)
*Zerui Qin,Sheng Yue,Xingyuan Hua,Yongjian Fu,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可执行智能体记忆（EAM），一种结构化知识图谱方法，将GUI规划从自由形式生成转变为鲁棒的检索-执行过程，通过状态感知DFS和动作组挖掘构建记忆，并利用值引导的图搜索（轻量Q函数模型引导MCTS）实现高效规划。在AndroidWorld上，EAM相比UI-TARS-7B提升19.6%，token成本较GPT-4o降低6倍，平均延迟2.8秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GUI智能体通常采用以模型为中心、逐步交互的范式，LLM需要在每个屏幕重新解释UI并重新决定动作，这在长程任务中表现脆弱。

Method: 1) 构建可执行智能体记忆（EAM），即结构化知识图谱；2) 使用状态感知DFS和动作组挖掘的高效记忆构建流水线；3) 引入值引导的图搜索，用轻量Q函数模型引导蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）在知识图谱上进行规划。

Result: 在AndroidWorld上，EAM比UI-TARS-7B提升19.6%，token成本相对于GPT-4o降低6倍，平均延迟2.8秒。

Conclusion: EAM实现了可靠、快速且支持长程任务的GUI自动化，并在理论上建立了Q模型的偏差一致性并推导了路径恢复的样本复杂度界。

Abstract: Modern GUI agents typically rely on a model-centric and step-wise interaction paradigm, where LLMs must re-interpret the UI and re-decide actions at every screen, which is fragile in long-horizon tasks. In this paper, we propose Executable Agentic Memory (EAM), a structured Knowledge Graph (KG) that shifts GUI planning from free-form generation to a robust retrieval-and-execution process. Our approach includes a sample-efficient memory construction pipeline using state-aware DFS and action-group mining to compress multi-step routines. To ensure efficient planning, we introduce a value-guided graph search where a lightweight Q-function model steers Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) over the KG. We theoretically establish bias-consistency for the Q-model and derive sample complexity bounds for path recovery. Empirically, EAM outperforms state-of-the-art baselines like UI-TARS-7B by up to $19.6\%$ on AndroidWorld, while reducing token costs $6\times$ relative to GPT-4o. With a $2.8$s average latency, EAM enables reliable, quick, and long-horizon GUI automation.

</details>


### [152] [LISA: Cognitive Arbitration for Signal-Free Autonomous Intersection Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12321)
*Abderrahmane Lakas,Mohamed Amine Ferrag,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LISA框架，利用大语言模型进行基于意图驱动的信号灯自由交叉口管理，相比传统方法在控制延迟（降低89.1%）、等待时间（降低93%）、队列长度（降低60.6%）和燃油消耗（降低48.8%）方面均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交叉口管理方法面临挑战：信号控制器无法感知车辆个体差异、基于预留的方法缺乏意图感知、基于LLM的系统仍依赖信号基础设施；LLM推理延迟限制其在亚秒级控制场景中的应用。需要一种将LLM作为主要决策者的信号灯自由管理方案。

Method: 提出LISA（LLM-Based Intent-Driven Speed Advisory）框架，一种信号灯自由的认知仲裁框架。利用LLM对车辆声明的意图进行推理，综合考虑优先级类别、队列压力和能源偏好，生成速度建议以实现无信号交叉口协调。

Result: 相比固定周期控制、SCATS、AIM和GLOSA：平均控制延迟降低89.1%，保持服务水平C（其他方法降为F）；接近饱和需求下，平均等待时间降低93%，峰值队列长度降低60.6%；燃油消耗降低48.8%；意图满意度达86.2%（最佳非LLM方法为61.2%）。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的推理能够实现实时、无信号灯的交叉口管理，为智能交通系统中的协同驾驶提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong potential for Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), particularly in tasks requiring situational reasoning and multi-agent coordination. These capabilities make them well suited for cooperative driving, where rule-based approaches struggle in complex and dynamic traffic environments. Intersection management remains especially challenging due to conflicting right-of-way demands, heterogeneous vehicle priorities, and vehicle-specific kinematic constraints that must be resolved in real time. However, existing approaches typically use LLMs as auxiliary components on top of signal-based systems rather than as primary decision-makers. Signal controllers remain vehicle-agnostic, reservation-based methods lack intent awareness, and recent LLM-based systems still depend on signal infrastructure. In addition, LLM inference latency limits their use in sub-second control settings.
  We propose LISA (LLM-Based Intent-Driven Speed Advisory), a signal-free cognitive arbitration framework for autonomous intersection management. LISA uses an LLM to reason over declared vehicle intents, incorporating priority classes, queue pressure, and energy preferences. We evaluate LISA against fixed-cycle control, SCATS, AIM, and GLOSA across varying traffic loads. Results show that LISA reduces mean control delay by up to 89.1% and maintains Level of Service C while all non-LLM baselines degrade to Level of Service F. Under near-saturated demand, LISA reduces mean waiting time by 93% and peak queue length by 60.6% relative to fixed-cycle control. It also lowers fuel consumption by up to 48.8% and achieves 86.2% intent satisfaction, compared to 61.2% for the best non-LLM method. These results demonstrate that LLM-based reasoning can enable real-time, signal-free intersection management.

</details>


### [153] [Towards Automated Air Traffic Safety Assessment Around Non-Towered Airports Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12332)
*Torsten Darrell,Mahyar Ghazanfari,Jordan Kam,Alexandre Bayen,Amin Tabrizian,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用视觉-语言模型（VLM）框架对非管制机场进行飞行后安全分析，结合CTAF无线电通信转录、METAR气象数据、ADS-B飞行轨迹和VFR航图等多模态数据；在Half Moon Bay机场的定性和定量评估表明，即使仅使用CTAF和METAR输入，开源LLM在二元分类任务上macro F1超过0.85，证明了VLM分析非管制机场安全的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非管制机场依赖CTAF进行空中交通协调，但由于飞行员自我通报的通信协议，常发生近乎空中相撞的事件，缺乏有效的飞行后安全分析手段。

Method: 提出通用VLM框架，整合CTAF无线电转录（自然语言）、METAR气象数据、ADS-B飞行轨迹和VFR航图；在Half Moon Bay机场进行真实案例定性评估（使用Gemini 2.5 Pro识别通行权违规），并基于12类别危险分类体系的合成数据集对三种开源（Qwen 2.5-7B、Mistral-7B、Gemma-2-9B）和三种闭源（GPT-4o、GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6）LLM进行定量基准测试。

Result: 真实案例中Gemini 2.5 Pro成功识别通行权违规；在仅使用CTAF和METAR输入的情况下，开源LLM在二元名义/危险分类任务上macro F1超过0.85。

Conclusion: VLM分析非管制机场安全具有潜在价值，未来工作包括跨所有模态的定量评估和更大规模真实案例验证。

Abstract: We investigate frameworks for post-flight safety analysis at non-towered airports using large language models (LLMs). Non-towered airports rely on the Common Traffic Advisory Frequency (CTAF) for air traffic coordination and experience frequent near mid-air collisions due to the pilot self-announcement communication protocol. We propose a general vision-language model (VLM) approach to analyze the transcribed CTAF radio communications in natural language, METeorological Aerodrome Report (METAR) weather data, Automatic Dependent Surveillance-Broadcast (ADS-B) flight trajectories, and Visual Flight Rules sectional charts of the airfield. We provide a preliminary study at Half Moon Bay Airport, with a qualitative real world case study and a quantitative evaluation using a new synthetic dataset of communications and weather modalities. We qualitatively evaluate our framework on real flight data using Gemini 2.5 Pro, demonstrating accurate identification of a right-of-way violation. The synthetic dataset is derived from real examples and includes a 12-category hazard taxonomy, and is used to benchmark three open-source (Qwen 2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B) and three closed-source (GPT-4o, GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6) LLM models on the subset of inputs related to CTAF and METAR. Even limited to CTAF and METAR inputs and open source LLMs, instances of our framework typically achieve a macro F1 score above 0.85 on a binary nominal/danger classification task. Future work includes a quantitative evaluation across all modalities and a larger number of real world examples. Taken together, our results suggest that VLM analysis of safety at non-towered airports may be a valuable future capability.

</details>


### [154] [Reinforcing VLAs in Task-Agnostic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12334)
*Yucen Wang,Rui Yu,Fengming Zhang,Junjie Lu,Xinyao Qin,Tianxiang Zhang,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAW-Dream提出了一种任务无关的世界模型和奖励模型框架，通过预训练的世界模型和现成的VLM实现零样本策略微调，无需任务特定数据即可适应新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在使用世界模型进行RL微调时，仍依赖任务特定数据来微调世界模型和奖励模型，限制了其扩展到未见任务的 scalability。

Method: 1) 使用预训练的任务无关世界模型预测未来轨迹；2) 利用现成的VLM生成奖励信号；3) 引入双噪声验证机制过滤不可靠的想象轨迹。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，RAW-Dream均取得了一致的性能提升，证明通用物理先验可以替代昂贵的任务依赖数据。

Conclusion: 任务无关的物理先验能够有效替代任务特定数据，为VLA模型的可扩展适应提供了高效路线图。

Abstract: Post-training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models via reinforcement learning (RL) in learned world models has emerged as an effective strategy to adapt to new tasks without costly real-world interactions. However, while using imagined trajectories reduces the sample complexity of policy training, existing methods still heavily rely on task-specific data to fine-tune both the world and reward models, fundamentally limiting their scalability to unseen tasks. To overcome this, we argue that world and reward models should capture transferable physical priors that enable zero-shot inference. We propose RAW-Dream (Reinforcing VLAs in task-Agnostic World Dreams), a new paradigm that completely disentangles world model learning from downstream task dependencies. RAW-Dream utilizes a world model pre-trained on diverse task-free behaviors for predicting future rollouts, and an off-the-shelf Vision-Language Model (VLM) for reward generation. Because both components are task-agnostic, VLAs can be readily finetuned for any new task entirely within this zero-shot imagination. Furthermore, to mitigate world model hallucinations, we introduce a dual-noise verification mechanism to filter out unreliable rollouts. Extensive experiments across simulation and real-world settings demonstrate consistent performance gains, proving that generalized physical priors can effectively substitute for costly task-dependent data, offering a highly scalable roadmap for VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [155] [Classifier Context Rot: Monitor Performance Degrades with Context Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12366)
*Sam Martin,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前前沿模型作为分类器时，在超过80万token的长上下文中，对编码智能体危险行为的漏检率比短上下文高出2-30倍，可通过周期性提示等技术部分缓解，但现有监控基准因未考虑长上下文退化而高估了监控性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体监控基准的转录本长度很少超过10万token，而实际编码智能体的行为日志常超过50万token，导致对长上下文中模型监控能力的评估存在盲区。

Method: 构建包含超长转录本（达80万token以上）的数据集，测试Opus 4.6、GPT 5.4、Gemini 3.1等前沿模型在不同长度上下文中识别细微危险行为的准确率，并尝试周期性提示等缓解技术。

Result: 在80万token的良性活动后发生的危险行为，模型的漏检率是短上下文场景的2-30倍；周期性提示可部分缓解此退化。

Conclusion: 当前监控评估因忽略长上下文退化而高估了实际性能，需将长上下文场景纳入标准评估体系，并通过更好的后训练进一步缓解该问题。

Abstract: Monitoring coding agents for dangerous behavior using language models requires classifying transcripts that often exceed 500K tokens, but prior agent monitoring benchmarks rarely contain transcripts longer than 100K tokens. We show that when used as classifiers, current frontier models fail to notice dangerous actions more often in longer transcripts. In particular, on a dataset that requires identifying when a coding agent takes a subtly dangerous action, Opus 4.6, GPT 5.4, and Gemini 3.1 miss these actions $2\times$ to $30\times$ more often when they occur after 800K tokens of benign activity than when they occur on their own. We also show that these weaknesses can be partially mitigated with prompting techniques such as periodic reminders throughout the transcript and may be mitigated further with better post-training. Monitor evaluations that do not consider long-context degradation are likely overestimating monitor performance.

</details>


### [156] [ProfiliTable: Profiling-Driven Tabular Data Processing via Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12376)
*Wei Liu,Yang Gu,Xi Yan,Zihan Nan,Beicheng Xu,Keyao Ding,Bin Cui,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProfiliTable是一个基于动态分析的多智能体框架，通过探索、知识增强合成和反馈驱动优化，自动化表格处理任务，在18种表格任务类型上优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的表格处理方法因模糊指令、复杂任务结构和缺乏结构化反馈，常生成语法正确但语义有缺陷的代码，难以可靠地处理真实数据管道中的表格处理任务。

Method: 提出ProfiliTable多智能体框架，包含：(i) Profiler：执行ReAct风格的数据探索以构建语义理解；(ii) Generator：检索精选算子合成任务感知代码；(iii) Evaluator-Summarizer循环：注入执行分数和诊断洞察实现闭环优化。三者协同构建并迭代精炼统一执行上下文。

Result: 在涵盖18种表格任务类型的多样化基准上，ProfiliTable持续优于强基线方法，尤其在复杂多步骤场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 动态分析在将模糊用户意图可靠转化为鲁棒且合规的表格变换中发挥关键作用。

Abstract: Table processing-including cleaning, transformation, augmentation, and matching-is a foundational yet error-prone stage in real-world data pipelines. While recent LLM-based approaches show promise for automating such tasks, they often struggle in practice due to ambiguous instructions, complex task structures, and the lack of structured feedback, resulting in syntactically correct but semantically flawed code. To address these challenges, we propose ProfiliTable, an autonomous multi-agent framework centered on dynamic profiling, which constructs and iteratively refines a unified execution context through interactive exploration, knowledge-augmented synthesis, and feedback-driven refinement. ProfiliTable integrates (i) a Profiler that performs ReAct-style data exploration to build semantic understanding, (ii) a Generator that retrieves curated operators to synthesize task-aware code, and (iii) an Evaluator-Summarizer loop that injects execution scores and diagnostic insights to enable closed-loop refinement. Extensive experiments on a diverse benchmark covering 18 tabular task types demonstrate that ProfiliTable consistently outperforms strong baselines, particularly in complex multi-step scenarios. These results highlight the critical role of dynamic profiling in reliably translating ambiguous user intents into robust and governance-compliant table transformations.

</details>


### [157] [Formalize, Don't Optimize: The Heuristic Trap in LLM-Generated Combinatorial Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12421)
*Haoyu Wang,Yuliang Song,Tao Li,Zhiwei Deng,Yaqing Wang,Deepak Ramachandran,Eldan Cohen,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构建包含100个组合问题的CP-SynC-XL基准，评估了三种求解器构建范式（原生Python、Python+OR-Tools、MiniZinc+OR-Tools），发现Python+OR-Tools正确率最高，而LLM自主进行搜索优化效果有限且常引入错误，建议将LLM主要用于形式化建模而非搜索优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在直接推理复杂组合问题时表现不佳，神经符号系统开始利用LLM合成可执行求解器，但关于求解器应如何表示以及是否应让LLM同时优化搜索过程，目前缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 构建CP-SynC-XL基准（100个组合问题，4577个实例），评估三种范式：原生算法搜索（Python）、通过Python求解器API的约束建模（Python+OR-Tools）、声明式约束建模（MiniZinc+OR-Tools）。通过代码级审计分析搜索优化提示的效果。

Result: Python+OR-Tools正确率最高；MiniZinc+OR-Tools覆盖率最低；原生Python最易输出格式正确但验证失败的解。搜索优化提示仅带来1.03-1.12x的中位数加速，呈双峰效应，且正确率在长尾问题上显著下降。代码审计发现三种范式各有特定的启发式陷阱。

Conclusion: 建议保守的设计原则：LLM应主要用于形式化变量、约束和目标供已验证的求解器使用，LLM自主编写的搜索优化在使用前需单独验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to solve complex combinatorial problems through direct reasoning, so recent neuro-symbolic systems increasingly use them to synthesize executable solvers. A central design question is how the LLM should represent the solver, and whether it should also attempt to optimize search. We introduce CP-SynC-XL, a benchmark of 100 combinatorial problems (4,577 instances), and evaluate three solver-construction paradigms: native algorithmic search (Python), constraint modeling through a Python solver API (Python + OR-Tools), and declarative constraint modeling (MiniZinc + OR-Tools). We find a consistent representational divergence: Python + OR-Tools attains the highest correctness across LLMs, while MiniZinc + OR-Tools has lower absolute coverage despite using the same OR-Tools back-end. Native Python is the most likely to return a schema-valid solution that fails verification, whereas solver-backed paths preserve higher conditional fidelity. On the heuristic axis, prompting for search optimization yields only small median speed-ups (1.03-1.12x) and a strongly bimodal effect: many instances slow down, and correctness drops sharply on a long tail of problems. A paired code-level audit traces these regressions to a recurring heuristic trap. Under an efficiency-oriented prompt, the LLM may replace complete search with local approximations (Python), inject unverified bounds (Python + OR-Tools), or add redundant declarative machinery that overwhelms or over-constrains the model (MiniZinc + OR-Tools). These findings support a conservative design principle for LLM-generated combinatorial solvers: use the LLM primarily to formalize variables, constraints, and objectives for verified solvers, and separately check any LLM-authored search optimization before use.

</details>


### [158] [CAAFC: Chronological Actionable Automated Fact-Checker for misinformation / non-factual hallucination detection and correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12436)
*Islam Eldifrawi,Shengrui Wang,Amine Trabelsi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAAFC框架，一种超越现有SOTA的自动化事实检测系统，能够检测和纠正事实错误与幻觉，并支持证据库的动态更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFC系统与专业事实核查人员的实际工作方式存在脱节，且无法有效处理AI生成的幻觉内容。

Method: CAAFC框架，能够处理声称、对话和讨论，通过主要信息来源提供可操作的论证来检测并纠正事实错误和幻觉，并动态更新证据和知识库。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上超越了SOTA的AFC和幻觉检测系统。

Conclusion: CAAFC通过引入可操作的论证纠正机制和动态证据更新能力，弥合了现有AFC系统与实际事实核查需求之间的差距，提升了事实验证的可靠性。

Abstract: With the vast amount of content uploaded every hour, along with the AI generated content that can include hallucinations, Automated Fact-Checking (AFC) has become increasingly vital, as it is infeasible for human fact-checkers to manually verify the sheer volume of information generated online. Professional fact-checkers have identified several gaps in existing AFC systems, noting a misalignment between how these systems operate and how fact-checking is performed in practice. In this paper, we introduce CAAFC (Chronological Actionable Automated Fact-Checker), a frame-work designed to bridge these gaps. It surpasses SOTA AFC and hallucination detection systems across multiple benchmark datasets. CAAFC operates on claims, conversations, and dialogues, enabling it not only to detect factual errors and hallucinations, but also to correct them by providing actionable justifications supported by primary information sources. Furthermore, CAAFC can update evidence and knowledge bases by incorporating recent and contextual information when necessary, thereby enhancing the reliability of fact verification.

</details>


### [159] [Towards Affordable Energy: A Gymnasium Environment for Electric Utility Demand-Response Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12462)
*Jose E. Aguilar Escamilla,Lingdong Zhou,Xiangqi Zhu,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DR-Gym，一个开源在线Gymnasium兼容环境，用于从电力公司角度训练和评估需求响应程序，解决了离线数据无法捕捉定价信号与用户反馈动态交互的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气和不稳定的批发电价使居民面临巨大财务风险，但配电级需求响应作为电网灵活性和能源可负担性的工具尚未充分利用；离线历史数据无法捕捉电力公司定价信号与客户接受及适应需求响应程序之间的动态交互反馈循环。

Method: 开发DR-Gym开源环境，包含：市场级电力公司视角的丰富观测空间、基于真实极端事件校准的机制转换批发价格模型、基于物理的建筑需求曲线、可配置多目标奖励函数，支持强化学习训练与评估。

Result: 通过基线策略和数据快照，验证了该模拟器能够创建真实且可学习的环境。

Conclusion: DR-Gym填补了现有设备级能源模拟器在电力公司市场级需求响应优化方面的空白，为利用强化学习优化需求响应顺序决策过程提供了可行的开源平台。

Abstract: Extreme weather and volatile wholesale electricity markets expose residential consumers to catastrophic financial risks, yet demand response at the distribution level remains an underutilized tool for grid flexibility and energy affordability. While a demand-response program can shield consumers by issuing financial credits during high-price periods, optimizing this sequential decision-making process presents a unique challenge for reinforcement learning despite the plentiful offline historical smart meter and wholesale pricing data available publicly. Offline historical data fails to capture the dynamic, interactive feedback loop between an electric utility's pricing signals and customer acceptance and adaptation to a demand-response program. To address this, we introduce DR-Gym, an open-source, online Gymnasium-compatible environment designed to train and evaluate demand-response from the electric utility's perspective. Unlike existing device-level energy simulators, our environment focuses on the market-level electric utility setting and provides a rich observational space relevant to the electric utility. The simulator additionally features a regime-switching wholesale price model calibrated to real-world extreme events, alongside physics-based building demand profiles. For our learning signal, we use a configurable, multi-objective reward function for specifying diverse learning objectives. We demonstrate through baseline strategies and data snapshots the capability of our simulator to create realistic and learnable environments.

</details>


### [160] [ToolCUA: Towards Optimal GUI-Tool Path Orchestration for Computer Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12481)
*Xuhao Hu,Xi Zhang,Haiyang Xu,Kyle Qiao,Jingyi Yang,Xuanjing Huang,Jing Shao,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToolCUA 是一个端到端智能体，通过分阶段训练范式（轨迹扩展、引导式强化微调、在线强化学习）学习最优 GUI-工具路径选择，在 OSWorld-MCP 上达到 46.85% 准确率，较基线提升约 66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUA）在混合动作空间（原子 GUI 动作与高层工具调用）中常不确定何时切换，导致次优执行路径。根本原因在于高质量的交替 GUI-工具轨迹稀缺、真实工具轨迹收集成本高且脆弱、以及缺乏轨迹级别的路径选择监督信号。

Method: 提出 ToolCUA，包含三阶段训练范式：(1) 交替 GUI-工具轨迹扩展管线，利用静态 GUI 轨迹合成接地工具库生成多样化交替轨迹；(2) 工具引导的 GUI RFT，结合热身监督微调与单步强化学习，优化关键切换点的决策；(3) 在线智能体强化学习，在高保真 GUI-工具环境中以工具高效路径奖励引导适当工具使用和更短路径。

Result: 在 OSWorld-MCP 上达到 46.85% 准确率，较基线提升约 66%，在可比规模模型中达到最新最优；较纯 GUI 设置提升 3.9%，验证了有效 GUI-工具编排能力。

Conclusion: 在混合动作空间中进行训练是实现真实世界数字智能体的有前景范式。

Abstract: Computer Use Agents (CUAs) can act through both atomic GUI actions, such as click and type, and high-level tool calls, such as API-based file operations, but this hybrid action space often leaves them uncertain about when to continue with GUI actions or switch to tools, leading to suboptimal execution paths. This difficulty stems from the scarcity of high-quality interleaved GUI-Tool trajectories, the cost and brittleness of collecting real tool trajectories, and the lack of trajectory-level supervision for GUI-Tool path selection. In this paper, we propose ToolCUA, an end-to-end agent designed to learn optimal GUI-Tool path selection through a staged training paradigm. We first introduce an Interleaved GUI-Tool Trajectory Scaling Pipeline that repurposes abundant static GUI trajectories and synthesizes a grounded tool library, enabling diverse GUI-Tool trajectories without manual engineering or real tool-trajectory collection. We then perform Tool-Bootstrapped GUI RFT, combining warmup SFT with single-turn RL to improve decisions at critical GUI-Tool switching points. Finally, we optimize ToolCUA with Online Agentic RL in a high-fidelity GUI-Tool environment, guided by a Tool-Efficient Path Reward that encourages appropriate tool use and shorter execution paths. Experiments on OSWorld-MCP show that ToolCUA achieves 46.85% accuracy, a relative improvement of approximately 66% over the baseline, establishing a new state of the art among models of comparable scale. It also improves by 3.9% over GUI-only settings, demonstrating effective GUI-Tool orchestration. The results further suggest that training in a hybrid action space is a promising paradigm for real-world digital agents. Open-sourced here: https://x-plug.github.io/ToolCUA/

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [PASA: A Principled Embedding-Space Watermarking Approach for LLM-Generated Text under Semantic-Invariant Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10977)
*Zhenxin Ai,Haiyun He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PASA是一种基于语义层面的鲁棒且无损的LLM水印算法，通过潜在嵌入空间中的语义簇和共享随机性机制实现水印嵌入与检测，在检测精度、鲁棒性和文本质量之间取得最优权衡，尤其能抵抗 paraphrasing 等语义不变攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印方法易受语义不变攻击（如 paraphrasing）影响，需要一种在语义层面嵌入水印的鲁棒算法。

Method: PASA在潜在嵌入空间中对语义簇进行操作，通过共享随机性（由密钥和语义历史同步）构建token序列与辅助序列之间的分布依赖关系，其设计基于理论框架中的联合最优嵌入-检测对，实现检测精度、鲁棒性和失真之间的基本权衡。

Result: 在多个LLM和语义不变攻击下，PASA在强 paraphrasing 攻击下仍保持鲁棒性，同时保持高文本质量，优于标准词汇空间基线方法。消融研究验证了超参数选择的有效性。

Conclusion: PASA在语义层面实现了鲁棒且无损的水印，达到了检测精度、鲁棒性和文本质量之间的最优权衡，有效抵抗语义不变攻击。

Abstract: Watermarking for large language models (LLMs) is a promising approach for detecting LLM-generated text and enabling responsible deployment. However, existing watermarking methods are often vulnerable to semantic-invariant attacks, such as paraphrasing. We propose PASA, a principled, robust, and distortion-free watermarking algorithm that embeds and detects a watermark at the semantic level. PASA operates on semantic clusters in a latent embedding space and constructs a distributional dependency between token and auxiliary sequences via shared randomness synchronized by a secret key and semantic history. This design is grounded in our theoretical framework that characterizes a jointly optimal embedding-detection pair, achieving the fundamental trade-offs among detection accuracy, robustness, and distortion. Evaluations across multiple LLMs and semantic-invariant attacks demonstrate that PASA remains robust even under strong paraphrasing attacks while preserving high text quality, outperforming standard vocabulary-space baselines. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of our hyperparameter choices. Webpage: https://ai-kunkun.github.io/PASA_page/.

</details>


### [162] [Few-Shot Truly Benign DPO Attack for Jailbreaking LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10998)
*Sangyeon Yoon,Wonje Jeung,Yoonjun Cho,Dongjae Jeon,Albert No*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现，仅使用10个无害偏好对（偏好有帮助回答而非拒绝回答）对LLM进行DPO微调，即可显著削弱模型的安全对齐，导致模型在有害提示上也拒绝回答能力大幅下降。攻击成本低至0.1-1.7美元，且数据在表面上与正常减少过度拒绝的微调请求无法区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注SFT对安全对齐的削弱，但偏好优化（如DPO）的安全风险尚未被充分理解。本文旨在揭示DPO在微调API中存在的隐蔽且更强的安全威胁。

Method: 作者构建了仅10个无害偏好对，每个对包含一个良性提示、一个正常有帮助回答（作为偏好回答）和一个拒绝回答（作为非偏好回答）。使用OpenAI支持的DPO微调API对GPT-4o、GPT-4.1等模型进行微调，并在开源模型上探索更少数据的效果。

Result: 在GPT-4o上攻击成功率59.13%，GPT-4.1上70.20%，GPT-4.1-mini上54.80%，GPT-4.1-nano上81.73%，成本分别仅1.7、1.7、0.3、0.1美元。开源模型上，甚至单个偏好对即可产生效果。

Conclusion: DPO微调API存在严重安全漏洞，极少量看似无害的偏好数据即可有效绕过安全对齐，且数据本身难以被审计识别为恶意，对LLM部署安全构成实质性威胁。

Abstract: Fine-tuning APIs make frontier LLMs easy to customize, but they can also weaken safety alignment during fine-tuning. While prior work shows that benign supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can reduce refusal behavior, deployed fine-tuning pipelines increasingly support preference-based objectives, whose safety risks remain less understood. We show that Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) introduces a stronger and harder-to-audit failure mode. We propose a truly benign DPO attack using only 10 harmless preference pairs, the minimum data scale accepted by OpenAI's fine-tuning service. Each pair contains a benign prompt, a normal helpful answer as the preferred response, and a refusal as the dispreferred response. Unlike prior benign fine-tuning attacks, our data exhibits no suspicious behavior: it is practically indistinguishable from the fine-tuning request of a legitimate user seeking to reduce over-refusal, making harmful intent almost impossible to infer from the request alone. Nevertheless, because DPO directly optimizes the model to prefer helpful answers over refusals, this seemingly benign objective broadly suppresses refusal behavior and transfers to harmful prompts outside the fine-tuning data. Across OpenAI models supporting DPO fine-tuning, our attack achieves attack success rates of 59.13% on GPT-4o, 70.20% on GPT-4.1, 54.80% on GPT-4.1-mini, and 81.73% on GPT-4.1-nano, at costs of only \$1.7, \$1.7, \$0.3, and \$0.1. Moreover, on open-weight models that do not impose minimum data requirements, we find that this effect can emerge from even a single benign preference pair.

</details>


### [163] [MT-JailBench: A Modular Benchmark for Understanding Multi-Turn Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11002)
*Xinkai Zhang,Zhipeng Wei,Huanli Gong,Jing Ting Zheng,Yuchen Zhang,Yue Dong,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-JailBench，一个模块化评估框架，用于在固定条件下公平比较多轮越狱攻击方法。通过将攻击分解为五个交互模块，发现资源预算和评估函数是主要混淆因素，提示生成模块贡献了大部分性能差异，而动态策略生成并非必要；重组最优组件可产生更强的攻击配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估中，多轮越狱方法通常以黑盒管道形式评估，由于预算、评判器、重试规则和策略生成过程不同，难以区分报告的性能提升来自更强的攻击机制还是不同的实验条件。

Method: 提出MT-JailBench模块化框架，将每次攻击分解为五个模块：评估函数、攻击策略、提示生成、提示精炼和流程控制，实现攻击方法间的公平比较和组件级分析。

Result: 1）资源预算和评估函数是主要混淆因素，控制这些变量会显著改变攻击排名；2）提示生成模块贡献了大部分性能差异，精炼和流程控制提供中等增益；3）显式动态策略生成并非总是必要，固定策略的随机采样可媲美复杂多样化机制；4）重组最优组件可产生超越源攻击的强攻击配置。

Conclusion: MT-JailBench提供了一个模块化框架，用于比较多轮越狱攻击、理解各组件影响，并指导更强的红队评估。

Abstract: Multi-turn jailbreaks exploit the ability of large language models to accumulate and act on conversational context. Instead of stating a harmful request directly, an attacker can gradually steer the conversation toward an unsafe answer. Recent methods demonstrate this risk, but they are usually evaluated as black-box pipelines with different budgets, judges, retry rules, and strategy generation procedures. As a result, it is often unclear whether reported gains reflect stronger attack mechanisms or different experimental conditions. We introduce MT-JailBench, a modular evaluation framework for benchmarking multi-turn jailbreaks under fixed conditions. MT-JailBench implements each attack as five interacting modules: evaluation function, attack strategy, prompt generation, prompt refinement, and flow control. This design enables fair comparison across attack methods and component-wise analysis of what drives attack success. Using MT-JailBench, we find that resource budgets and evaluation functions are major confounders: controlling turns, retries, interactions, sampled strategies, and judges substantially change the ranking of attacks. At the component level, prompt generation accounts for most performance variation, while refinement and flow control provide moderate gains. We also find that explicit dynamic strategy generation is not always necessary; stochastic sampling from a fixed strategy can rival more elaborate diversification mechanisms. Finally, recomposing the best components yields a strong attack configuration that outperforms its source attacks and generalizes across diverse target LLMs. MT-JailBench therefore provides a modular framework for comparing multi-turn jailbreaks, understanding the impact of components, and guiding stronger red-teaming evaluations.

</details>


### [164] [The Authorization-Execution Gap Is a Major Safety and Security Problem in Open-World Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11003)
*Baoyuan Wu,Qingshan Liu,Adel Bibi,Irwin King,Siwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证授权-执行鸿沟（AEG）是开放世界智能体面临的主要安全与安保问题。AEG指主体意图授权与实际执行之间的偏差，源于委托层面不完整性、通道层面污染和组合层面碎片化三个结构性来源。论文主张应进行面向来源的诊断与防御，并在执行过程中实施授权完整性检查，而非仅依赖一次性前置过滤或事后审计。对NeurIPS的启示是，开放世界智能体论文应报告过程级证据，展示AEG在何处被检测、约束并归因于结构性来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界智能体自主执行过程中，授权意图与实际执行之间存在偏差（AEG），这种偏差即使很小也可能造成难以或无法撤销的损害。现有研究缺乏对AEG结构性来源的系统识别与针对性防御。

Method: 通过理论分析将AEG归因于三个结构性来源：委托层面不完整性（授权范围界定不足）、通道层面污染（通信信道被篡改或欺骗）和组合层面碎片化（多组件/多智能体交互中的授权碎片化）。主张基于来源的诊断与防御，并强调执行过程中动态授权完整性检查的必要性。

Result: 识别出AEG的三个结构性来源，并论证仅针对症状的防御无法解决根本原因。提出NeurIPS论文应报告过程级证据而非仅结果级指标。

Conclusion: 开放世界智能体的安全与安保应强调面向来源的诊断与防御策略，在执行过程中实施授权完整性检查。论文应报告过程级证据以提升领域研究的可复现性和可诊断性。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the Authorization-Execution Gap (AEG) is a major safety and security problem in open-world agents. The AEG is the divergence between what a principal intends to authorize and what an open-world agent ultimately executes. Because such agents act autonomously across tools, persistent state, and multi-agent handoffs, even small instances of authorization divergence can cause harm that is difficult or impossible to undo. We argue that many observed agent failures can be traced to three structural sources of AEG: delegation-level incompleteness, channel-level corruption, and composition-level fragmentation. The same observed failure may arise from any of these sources. Without identifying the source, a defense targeting the symptom alone cannot address the underlying cause. Agent safety and security should therefore emphasize source-oriented diagnosis and defense. Because the structural sources of AEG arise dynamically during execution, this approach necessarily requires authorization integrity checks applied during execution, rather than relying solely on one-shot upfront filtering or post-hoc audit. For NeurIPS, the implication is that papers on open-world agents should report not only outcome-level metrics such as task success or attack resistance, but also process-level evidence showing where AEG was detected, constrained, and attributed to a structural source during execution.

</details>


### [165] [DCVD: Dual-Channel Cross-Modal Fusion for Joint Vulnerability Detection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11015)
*Wenxin Tang,Wenbin Li,Junliang Liu,Jingyu Xiao,Xi Xiao,Mingzhe Liu,Jinlong Yang,Xuan Liu,Yuehe Ma,Wang Luo,Qing Li,Lei Wang,Peng Xiangli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DCVD是一个双通道跨模态漏洞检测框架，通过并行提取控制依赖与语义特征、对比对齐与双向交叉注意力融合、以及函数级与语句级联合监督，在真实漏洞基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞检测方法存在两个问题：(1) 依赖单一信息源（序列、结构或语义），未能联合利用跨模态互补优势；(2) 语句级定位仅作为函数级检测的副产品，缺乏显式的行级监督信号。

Method: DCVD通过双通道并行分支分别提取控制依赖特征和语义特征，利用对比对齐与双向交叉注意力机制融合跨模态表示，并引入函数级和语句级显式监督信号进行协同优化。

Result: 在大规模真实漏洞基准上，DCVD在函数级检测和语句级定位任务上均持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 提出的双通道跨模态融合与多粒度联合监督策略能有效提升漏洞检测与定位性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Software vulnerability detection plays a critical role in ensuring system security, where real-world auditing requires not only determining whether a function is vulnerable but also pinpointing the specific lines responsible. However, existing approaches either rely on a single information source -- sequential, structural, or semantic -- failing to jointly exploit the complementary strengths across modalities, or treat statement-level localization merely as a byproduct of function-level detection without explicit line-level supervision. To address these limitations, we propose DCVD (Dual-Channel Cross-Modal Vulnerability Detection), a unified framework that performs joint function-level detection and statement-level localization. DCVD extracts control-dependency and semantic features through two parallel branches and integrates them via contrastive alignment coupled with bidirectional cross-attention, effectively bridging the cross-modal representation gap. It further introduces explicit supervision signals at both the function and statement levels, enabling collaborative optimization across the two granularities. Extensive experiments on a large-scale real-world vulnerability benchmark demonstrate that DCVD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods on both function-level detection and statement-level localization. Our code is available at https://github.com/vinsontang1/DCVD.

</details>


### [166] [A Multi-Interface Firmware Acquisition and Validation Methodology for Low-Cost Consumer Drones: A Case Study on Three Holy Stone Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11040)
*Sandesh More,Sneha Sudhakaran,Marco Carvalho*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对三款Holy Stone消费级无人机（HS175D、HS720、HS360S）进行了固件提取与验证的系统性研究，评估了四种低成本的固件获取方法，并提出了一个三层验证框架（滑动窗口香农熵分析+binwalk结构签名分析+EMBA静态分析）来区分有效固件与伪成功提取结果。实验表明，验证通过的固件包含可识别的操作系统组件、存在已知CVE漏洞的老化库栈，且缺乏二进制加固机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级无人机嵌入式固件对安全社区几乎不可见，尤其是入门级机型缺乏保护机制且无公开文档，导致安全评估空白。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供可复现的固件获取与验证基线。

Method: 1) 四种固件提取方法：SPI Flash在线读取、SWD/JTAG调试端口访问、UART启动信息捕获、免拆芯片夹持接触法。2) 三层验证框架：滑动窗口香农熵分析（检测数据随机性/压缩特征）、binwalk结构签名分析（识别文件系统/固件头部）、EMBA框架静态分析（确认OS组件、CVE漏洞库、二进制加固缺失）。

Result: 验证通过的固件镜像包含可识别的操作系统组件、带有已知CVE暴露的老化库栈，且未启用任何二进制加固机制（如ASLR、栈保护、PIE等）。四种方法在成功率、镜像完整性和操作实用性上各有优劣，免拆夹持法避免了芯片拆焊风险。

Conclusion: 该研究为消费级无人机领域的固件重托管、漏洞分析、安全启动评估和嵌入式系统教育提供了可复现的基线方法和固件语料库。低成本的工具链即可实现有效的固件安全评估。

Abstract: Consumer unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) have evolved into capable computing platforms, yet their embedded firmware remains largely inaccessible to the security community. Entry-level models, in particular those marketed to first-time and younger operators, commonly ship with limited protection mechanisms and no public documentation of their software internals. This paper presents a systematic study of firmware extraction and validation applied to three Holy Stone consumer drone models: the HS175D, HS720, and HS360S. Rather than pursuing reverse-engineering outcomes, the work focuses on obtaining reliable, ground-truth firmware images across heterogeneous hardware designs using only commercially available, low-cost tooling. Four acquisition methods are evaluated SPI flash in-circuit reading, SWD/JTAG debug-port access, UART boot-message capture, and a clip-based contact approach that avoids chip desoldering and each is assessed for success rate, image completeness, and operational practicality. Post-acquisition quality is evaluated through sliding-window Shannon entropy profiling and structural-signature analysis using binwalk, together forming a three-tier validation framework that distinguishes validated images from those that appear successful at the tool level but contain no meaningful firmware content. Static analysis via the EMBA framework confirms that validated images contain identifiable OS components, aging library stacks with known CVE exposure, and no binary-hardening mechanisms. The resulting corpus and methodology provide a reproducible baseline for firmware rehosting, vulnerability analysis, secure-boot assessment, and embedded-systems education within the consumer UAV domain.
  Index Terms: consumer UAV, drone firmware, embedded systems security, entropy analysis, firmware extraction, IoT security, SPI flash, SWD/JTAG, UART.

</details>


### [167] [Red-Teaming Agent Execution Contexts: Open-World Security Evaluation on OpenClaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11047)
*Hongwei Yao,Yiming Liu,Yiling He,Bingrun Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepTrap框架，用于自动发现OpenClaw（一种智能语言模型系统）中的上下文漏洞。通过将对抗性上下文操纵建模为黑盒轨迹级优化问题，结合风险条件评估、多目标轨迹评分、奖励引导束搜索和反思式深度探测，在42个基准案例上评估9个目标模型，证明上下文妥协能诱发不安全行为同时保持用户任务完成，揭示了执行中心安全评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能语言模型系统越来越多地依赖可变执行上下文（包括文件、内存、工具、技能和辅助工件），这产生了超越显式用户提示的安全风险，现有方法缺乏对这类上下文漏洞的自动化发现机制。

Method: DeepTrap将对抗性上下文操纵形式化为黑盒轨迹级优化问题，平衡风险实现、良性任务保持和隐蔽性。结合风险条件评估、多目标轨迹评分、奖励引导束搜索和反思式深度探测来识别高价值的受损上下文。

Result: 构建了涵盖6类漏洞和7种操作场景的42个基准案例，评估9个目标模型。结果表明上下文妥协能在保持用户任务完成的同时诱发大量不安全行为，证明了仅依赖最终响应评估是不充分的。

Conclusion: 研究强调了智能AI系统需要以执行为中心的安全评估范式，而非仅关注最终输出。

Abstract: Agentic language-model systems increasingly rely on mutable execution contexts, including files, memory, tools, skills, and auxiliary artifacts, creating security risks beyond explicit user prompts. This paper presents DeepTrap, an automated framework for discovering contextual vulnerabilities in OpenClaw. DeepTrap formulates adversarial context manipulation as a black-box trajectory-level optimization problem that balances risk realization, benign-task preservation, and stealth. It combines risk-conditioned evaluation, multi-objective trajectory scoring, reward-guided beam search, and reflection-based deep probing to identify high-value compromised contexts. We construct a 42-case benchmark spanning six vulnerability classes and seven operational scenarios, and evaluate nine target models using attack and utility grading scores. Results show that contextual compromise can induce substantial unsafe behavior while preserving user-facing task completion, demonstrating that final-response evaluation is insufficient. The findings highlight the need for execution-centric security evaluation of agentic AI systems. Our code is released at: https://github.com/ZJUICSR/DeepTrap

</details>


### [168] [FedSurrogate: Backdoor Defense in Federated Learning via Layer Criticality and Surrogate Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11122)
*Fatima Z. Abacha,Sin G. Teo,Yuanxiang Wu,Lucas C. Cordeiro,Mustafa A. Mustafa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedSurrogate是一种通过双向梯度对齐过滤与层自适应异常检测相结合的后门防御方法，能在非独立同分布数据下将假阳性率控制在10%以下，攻击成功率低于2.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习后门防御方法在非独立同分布数据下存在严重的假阳性问题，错误地将良性客户端标记为恶意，导致模型精度显著下降。

Method: FedSurrogate结合双向梯度对齐过滤与层自适应异常检测。首先通过方向散度分析识别安全关键层进行选择性聚类，将检测信号集中在低维子空间；然后通过双向软过滤筛选可信客户端并挽救假阳性样本；最后用结构相似良性客户端的缩放替代更新替换恶意更新，保留梯度多样性的同时消除攻击影响。

Result: 在非独立同分布设置下，FedSurrogate在所有数据集和攻击类型上维持低于10%的假阳性率（对比基线方法为31-32%），主任务准确率更优，攻击成功率低于2.1%。

Conclusion: FedSurrogate通过软过滤与替代更新策略有效解决了非独立同分布数据下的假阳性问题，在保持高防御效果的同时显著降低了对良性客户端的误判。

Abstract: Federated Learning remains highly susceptible to backdoor attacks--malicious clients inject targeted behaviours into the global model. Existing defenses suffer from substantial false-positive rates under realistic non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data, incorrectly flagging benign clients and degrading model accuracy even when adversaries are correctly identified. We present FedSurrogate, a novel backdoor defense that addresses this limitation by combining bidirectional gradient alignment filtering with layer-adaptive anomaly detection. FedSurrogate performs selective clustering on security-critical layers identified via directional divergence analysis, concentrating the detection signal on a low-dimensional subspace. A bidirectional soft-filtering stage screens trusted clients for residual contamination while rescuing false positives from suspects, substantially reducing misclassifications under heterogeneous conditions. Rather than removing confirmed malicious updates, FedSurrogate replaces them with downscaled surrogate updates from structurally similar benign clients, preserving gradient diversity while neutralising adversarial influence. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FedSurrogate maintains false-positive rates below 10% across all datasets and attack types, compared to 31-32% for the nearest comparably effective baseline, while achieving superior main-task accuracy and maintaining attack success rates below 2.1% across all tested datasets and attack types under challenging non-IID settings.

</details>


### [169] [Benchmarking LLM-Based Static Analysis for Secure Smart Contract Development: Reliability, Limitations, and Potential Hybrid Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11163)
*Stefan-Claudiu Susan,Andrei Arusoaie,Dorel Lucanu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了大语言模型（LLM）作为智能合约安全审计工具的可靠性，发现其存在词汇偏见和外部数据验证不足的问题，导致高误报率，且提示技巧在精确率和召回率之间存在权衡。研究表明，LLM目前只能作为传统静态分析工具的补充，而非替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链交易的不可逆性使得智能合约漏洞识别对系统安全开发至关重要。尽管LLM越来越多地融入开发者工作流程，但其作为自主安全审计工具的可靠性尚未得到验证。本文旨在评估当前生成模型是否能够替代或仅作为传统静态分析工具的补充。

Method: 通过自定义自动化框架对LLM进行智能合约漏洞审计测试，评估其输出分类的准确性（框架分类准确率达92%）。分析词汇偏见（如标识符命名等非语义启发式）和外部数据输入验证不足对模型性能的影响，并探究不同提示技巧下精确率与召回率之间的权衡关系。

Result: LLM效能受到固有词汇偏见和外部数据输入验证不足的损害。依赖非语义启发式（如标识符命名）导致高误报率；不同提示技巧在精确率和召回率之间存在权衡。自定义自动化框架在对模型输出分类时达到92%的准确率。

Conclusion: 当前LLM作为智能合约安全审计工具的可靠性尚不足以替代传统静态分析工具，更可能作为辅助补充工具使用。其词汇偏见和验证不足的问题需进一步解决才能提升在安全审计领域的实用价值。

Abstract: The irreversible nature of blockchain transactions makes the identification of smart contract vulnerabilities an essential requirement for secure system development. While Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into developer workflows, their reliability as autonomous security auditors remains unproven. We assess whether current generative models are a viable replacement for, or only a complement to, traditional static-analysis tools.
  Our findings indicate that LLM efficacy is undermined by both inherent lexical bias and a lack of rigorous validation of external data inputs. This reliance on non-semantic heuristics, such as identifier naming, leads to a high frequency of false positives. Furthermore, prompting techniques reveal a trade-off between precision and recall. These results were derived using our custom automated framework, which achieves 92% accuracy in classifying model outputs.

</details>


### [170] [Adversarial SQL Injection Generation with LLM-Based Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11188)
*Ali Karakoc,H. Birkan Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于LLM的SQL注入攻击生成系统（RADAGAS和RefleXQLi），在240个实验、24万条载荷和220万次测试中，RADAGAS-GPT4o以22.73%的绕过率最优，对AI/ML型WAF绕过率高达92.49%，但对规则型WAF仅0-5.70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQL注入仍是OWASP Top 10严重威胁，而LLM技术的进步为自动化生成对抗性攻击测试以评估防御机制提供了新机遇，因此需要系统性地评估LLM在SQL注入生成方面的能力。

Method: 提出RADAGAS（检索增强生成对抗SQLi）和RefleXQLi（反思链式思维SQLi）两种LLM系统，使用GPT-4o、Claude 3.7 Sonnet和DeepSeek R1模型，对6种规则型、2种AI/ML型和2种商业WAF进行测试，共240个实验生成24万条载荷并执行220万次测试。

Result: RADAGAS-GPT4o以22.73%绕过率最优；RADAGAS变体对AI/ML型WAF绕过率极高（WAF-Brain达92.49%，CNN-WAF达80.48%），但对规则型WAF（ModSecurity和Coraza）仅0-5.70%；多样性较低的载荷更容易绕过但初始失败则整体效果差。

Conclusion: LLM在安全测试中具有巨大潜力，尤其针对AI/ML型WAF效果显著，但规则型WAF仍能有效抵御；为安全测试中LLM应用提供了全面视角。

Abstract: SQL injection (SQLi) attacks are still one of the serious attacks ranked in the Open Worldwide Application Security Project (OWASP) Top 10 threats. Today, with advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI), especially in Large Language Models (LLMs), an opportunity has been created for automating adversarial attack tests to measure the defense mechanisms. In this paper, we aim to create a comprehensive evaluation of use cases that utilize LLMs for adversarial SQL injection generation. We introduce two novel LLM-based systems, Retrieval Augmented Generation for Adversarial SQLi (RADAGAS) and Reflective Chain-of-Thought SQLi (RefleXQLi), and compare them with existing baselines against 10 Web Application Firewalls (WAFs) and one execution-based MySQL validator. To perform a comprehensive test, we used six rule-based open-source WAFs (ModSecurity PL1--3, Coraza PL1--3), 2 AI/ML-based WAFs (WAF Brain, CNN-WAF), and 2 commercial WAFs (AWS WAF and Cloudflare WAF). For the LLM models, we used GPT-4o, Claude 3.7 Sonnet, and DeepSeek R1. Our tests consist of 240 experiments that generate 240,000 payloads and perform 2.2 million tests against WAFs. Our comprehensive evaluation reveals that RADAGAS-GPT4o outperforms other baseline models with a 22.73\% bypass rate. The proposed RADAGAS variants are highly successful on AI/ML-based WAFs (92.49\% on WAF-Brain by RADAGAS-DeepSeek, 80.48\% on CNN-WAF by RADAGAS-Claude), but struggle to bypass rule-based WAFs (0--5.70\% on ModSecurity and Coraza). In addition to these findings, another observation is that creating less diverse payloads achieves more bypasses, however they show poor results if the initially chosen payload is not successful. We observe that our findings provide a comprehensive view on using LLM-based approaches in security testing.

</details>


### [171] [Continuous Discovery of Vulnerabilities in LLM Serving Systems with Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11202)
*Yunze Zhao,Yibo Zhao,Yuchen Zhang,Zaoxing Liu,Michelle L. Mazurek*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GRIEF是一个针对LLM推理引擎的灰盒模糊测试工具，通过多请求时序轨迹发现并发服务中的安全漏洞，已在vLLM和SGLang中发现15个漏洞，包括KV缓存隔离失效、跨请求性能干扰等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM推理系统安全性评估仅关注模型行为或单请求API测试，忽略了并发服务层（如KV缓存、批处理、前缀共享、推测解码、多租户调度）的共享状态行为带来的安全风险，这些风险只在真实并发工作负载下才显现。

Method: 提出GRIEF灰盒模糊测试框架：(1) 将带时序的多请求轨迹作为一等输入；(2) 使用轻量级oracle检测崩溃、挂起、性能异常和静默输出损坏；(3) 通过受控重放和对数概率检查确认可复现的服务层故障。

Result: 在vLLM和SGLang上发现15个漏洞，10个被开发者确认，包括2个CVE；涵盖KV缓存隔离失效、跨请求性能干扰、崩溃/活性缺陷。

Conclusion: 并发、缓存和状态重用会导致静默跨请求污染、噪声邻居拒绝服务以及延迟崩溃，无需恶意构造输入或显式服务错误即可触发；并发服务行为应成为LLM基础设施的一等安全和可靠性边界。

Abstract: LLM inference and serving systems have become security-critical infrastructure; however, many of their most concerning failures arise from the serving layer rather than from model behavior alone. Modern inference engines combine KV cache, batching, prefix sharing, speculative decoding, adapters, and multi-tenant scheduling, creating shared-state behavior that only emerges under realistic concurrent workloads and is missed by standard model, safety, and API tests. We present GRIEF, a greybox fuzzer for LLM inference engines that treats timed multi-request traces as first-class inputs, uses lightweight oracles to detect crashes, hangs, performance pathologies, and silent output corruption, and applies controlled replay with log-probability checks to confirm reproducible serving-layer failures. Across early campaigns on vLLM and SGLang, GRIEF discovers 15 vulnerabilities, 10 confirmed by engine developers, including 2 CVEs, spanning KV-cache isolation failures, cross-request performance interference, and crash or liveness bugs. These results show that concurrency, caching, and state reuse can induce silent cross-request contamination, noisy-neighbor denial of service, and delayed crashes without malformed inputs or explicit server errors, making concurrent serving behavior a first-class security and reliability boundary for LLM infrastructure.

</details>


### [172] [Context-Aware Spear Phishing: Generative AI-Enabled Attacks Against Individuals via Public Social Media Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11268)
*Elham Pourabbas Vafa,Sayak Saha Roy,Shirin Nilizadeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何利用公开社交媒体数据和生成式AI自动化大规模、高度个性化的鱼叉式钓鱼攻击，并评估了现有防御机制的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示GenAI被滥用于自动化个性化钓鱼攻击的潜在威胁，以及当前防御机制在面对这种上下文感知攻击时的脆弱性。

Method: 提出模块化框架，结合多模态信号提取、沟通风格画像和七种攻击策略实例化；大规模多模型评估（涵盖数千封生成邮件和八项安全相关标准）；用户研究对比LLM生成攻击与真实钓鱼邮件；分析现有提示级防御机制及两种补充防御方法。

Result: GenAI生成的邮件在个性化、上下文关联和说服力上显著更高；用户研究显示LLM攻击在八个维度上均优于APWG eCrimeX真实钓鱼邮件，且引发的人类怀疑度更低；现有防御机制在面对上下文适应攻击时效果有限。

Conclusion: 需要设计明确考虑上下文滥用的平台级安全防护机制，以应对规模化、个性化的GenAI钓鱼攻击。

Abstract: We demonstrate how publicly available social-media data and generative AI (GenAI) can be misused to automate and scale highly personalized, context-aware spear-phishing campaigns. With minimal attacker effort, a small amount of public activity per target is sufficient for GenAI models to extract interests and contextual cues, producing persuasive messages that mirror a target's style while bypassing generic content-moderation safeguards. We introduce a modular framework that combines multimodal signal extraction, communication-style profiling, and attack-type instantiation across seven strategies (baiting, scareware, honey trap, tailgating, impersonation, quid pro quo, and personalized emotional exploitation). We conduct a large-scale, multi-model evaluation covering thousands of generated emails and eight security-relevant criteria, benchmarking against a corpus of real-world phishing messages. The GenAI-produced emails exhibit markedly higher personalization, contextual grounding, and persuasive leverage. Importantly, a complementary user study corroborates these results, revealing that LLM-generated attacks consistently outperform APWG eCrimeX emails across eight dimensions while eliciting lower suspicion among human recipients. Finally, we measure and analyze the behavior of existing proactive, prompt-level defense mechanisms, which incorporate adaptive mechanisms, as well as two complementary defense approaches-policy-augmented SOTA safeguard models and system-instruction chain-of-thought moderation. We document how these defenses respond to contextualized and adaptive attack prompts, underscoring the need for platform-level safeguards that explicitly account for contextualized abuse at scale.

</details>


### [173] [A Systematic Security Testing Approach for InterUSS-based environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11339)
*Henrique Curi de Miranda,Ágney Lopes Roth Ferraz,Wagner Comin Sonaglio,Lourenço Alves Pereira Júnior*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从维护者视角提出了针对InterUSS（UTM联邦生态系统）的安全测试方法，通过部署和分析实际基础设施，识别关键组件并基于mTLS、OAuth 2.0等标准开发安全测试，最终形成测试指南，填补了该领域安全测试的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTM联邦生态系统（如InterUSS）在实现无人机服务供应商（USSs）安全协调的同时，引入了基础设施层面的安全挑战，而当前研究对此探索不足。

Method: 部署并分析运行的InterUSS基础设施，识别关键组件，基于mTLS和OAuth 2.0等标准协议开发针对性安全测试，并将测试汇编为测试指南。

Result: 形成了一份测试指南，支持InterUSS生态系统中的组件验证和交互分析。

Conclusion: 本文提出的安全测试方法及测试指南填补了InterUSS联邦生态系统安全测试领域的研究空白，有助于提升UTM基础设施的安全性。

Abstract: Unmanned Traffic Management (UTM) federated ecosystems, such as InterUSS, enable secure coordination among UAS Service Suppliers (USSs). However, they bring up some security challenges at the infrastructure level that haven't been fully explored. This paper presents a security testing approach for InterUSS-based environments from the maintainer's perspective. By deploying and analyzing a working InterUSS infrastructure, we pinpoint key components and develop specific security tests aligned with established standards and protocols, such as mTLS and OAuth 2.0. We compiled these tests into a Testing Guide that aids both component validation and interaction analysis across InterUSS-based ecosystems, filling a gap in current research.

</details>


### [174] [Options, Not Clicks: Lattice Refinement for Consent-Driven MCP Authorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11360)
*Ying Li,Yanju Chen,Peiran Wang,Issac Khabra,Faysal Hossain Shezan,Yu Feng,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Conleash 是一种客户端中间件，通过风险格、策略引擎和用户决策反馈循环，实现对模型上下文协议中工具调用的边界范围授权，在984条真实轨迹上达到98.2%准确率，并能捕获99.4%的升级调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（全开/全关开关或不透明的LLM决策）无法处理危险调用参数，且容易导致用户同意疲劳，需要更细粒度的有意义的用户同意机制。

Method: 采用风险格（risk lattice）自动允许已知边界内的安全调用并标记风险升级；策略引擎执行用户定义的不变量；通过精炼循环将用户决策转化为可复用规则。

Result: 在984条真实轨迹上评估：准确率98.2%，捕获99.4%的风险升级调用，策略验证开销仅8.2毫秒；用户研究（N=16）显示参与者显著偏好Conleash的范围许可方式。

Conclusion: Conleash通过边界范围授权有效解决了工具调用中的用户同意问题，在保证安全性的同时降低了用户负担，提升了信任度。

Abstract: As Model Context Protocol adoption grows, securing tool invocations via meaningful user consent has become a critical challenge, as existing methods, broad always allow toggles or opaque LLM-based decisions, fail to account for dangerous call arguments and often lead to consent fatigue. In this work, we present Conleash, a client-side middleware that enforces boundary-scoped authorization by utilizing a risk lattice to auto-permit safe calls within known boundaries while escalating risks, a policy engine for user-defined invariants, and a refinement loop that converts user decisions into reusable rules. Evaluated on 984 real-world traces, Conleash achieved 98.2% accuracy, caught 99.4% of escalations, and added only 8.2 ms of overhead for policy verification; furthermore, in a user study where N=16, participants significantly preferred Conleash scoped permissions over traditional methods, citing higher trust and reduced prompting.

</details>


### [175] [FlowSteer: Prompt-Only Workflow Steering Exposes Planning-Time Vulnerabilities in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11514)
*Fanxiao Li,Jiaying Wu,Tingchao Fu,Natasha Jaques,Wei Zhou,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中规划-执行架构的安全漏洞，提出了FlowSteer攻击方法（通过提示词操纵工作流编排）和FlowGuard防御方法，揭示了工作流形成过程是新的安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统采用规划-执行架构时，提示词可以塑造智能体组织方式而不修改MAS基础设施，这暴露了工作流形成过程中的攻击面。作者旨在研究这种社会影响力探测攻击并开发相应防御。

Method: 通过社会影响力探测工作流，分析工作流位置对恶意信号的放大/抑制效应以及谄媚框架对下游智能体传递信号的影响；提出FlowSteer攻击方法（将漏洞先验转化为单一精心设计的提示词）和FlowGuard输入侧防御方法。

Result: FlowSteer相比朴素提示攻击成功率高55%，可跨MAS设置迁移，在黑盒拓扑推断下依然有效；FlowGuard可将恶意成功率降低34%同时保持提示效用。

Conclusion: 工作流形成是多智能体LLM系统的新安全前沿，需要从规划时安全角度审视智能体协调本身的攻防问题；仅检查生成的工作流不足以防御此类攻击。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) powered by large language models (LLMs) increasingly adopt planner--executor architectures, where planners convert prompts into subtasks, roles, dependencies, and routing paths. This flexibility enables adaptive coordination, but exposes an attack surface in workflow formation: prompts can shape agent organization without modifying MAS infrastructure. We study this risk through social influence probing workflows to identify high-impact subtasks and malicious-signal propagation. The analysis reveals two vulnerabilities: workflow position can amplify or suppress a malicious signal, and sycophantic framing makes downstream agents more likely to relay it. We translate these findings into FlowSteer, a prompt-only workflow steering attack that converts vulnerability priors into one crafted prompt. FlowSteer aligns a malicious signal with influential task components and guides replanning toward dependencies that preserve propagation. Experiments show that FlowSteer increases malicious success by up to 55% over naive prompting, transfers across MAS setups, and remains effective with black-box topology inference. As FlowSteer biases the planning signals that generate the workflow, MAS defenses that inspect only the generated workflow provide limited protection. As such, we introduce FlowGuard, an input-side defense that reduces malicious success by up to 34% while preserving prompt utility. Our results position workflow formation as a new safety frontier for multi-agent LLM systems, opening a planning-time security perspective on how agent coordination itself can be attacked and defended.

</details>


### [176] [PhishSigma++: Malicious Email Detection with Typed Entity Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11619)
*Shang Shang,Ruiqi Wang,Ruijie Qi,Hao Li,Yingxiao Xiang,Yepeng Yao,Zhengwei Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PhishSigma++，一种基于实体关系的恶意邮件检测器，通过提取40种实体类型和5种跨类型关系构建带类型邮件图，利用粒子群优化选择稀疏判别掩码，在29,142条消息上达到0.9675 F1，并在对抗性Good Word填充攻击下保持0.9579 F1，显著优于基于文本的基线模型，弥合了实验室与真实环境之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有钓鱼邮件检测器过度依赖可变的文本特征，在干净数据上表现良好，但在面对文本级对抗性操作时性能严重下降，存在实验室到真实环境的性能差距。攻击者利用AIGC获得了更强的语言能力和逃避技术，亟需挖掘对抗文本修改下不变的特征信号。

Method: PhishSigma++从RFC822邮件中提取40种带类型实体，计算5种跨类型关系构建带类型邮件图，使用粒子群优化（PSO）选择稀疏判别掩码，支持分类和类型级证据总结。该方法将Sigma规则的手工逻辑与学习到的关系掩码统一在单邮件框架中。

Result: 在29,142条消息上，PhishSigma++在干净数据上达到0.9675 F1；在非自适应Good Word填充攻击（ρ=0.8）下保持0.9579 F1，而基于令牌的贝叶斯滤波器降至0.0243，DistilBERT钓鱼检测点降至0.7284。相比传统Sigma规则，提供了更高的检测率、更广泛的关系不变性覆盖和数据驱动的特征选择。

Conclusion: 阈值化的带类型关系分数编码了Sigma风格字段条件的有用片段，将手工规则逻辑与学习到的关系掩码统一在单邮件框架中，有效弥合了实验室与真实环境之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Here is a further shortened version (pure text, no extra formatting, academic style preserved, no content change):
  Abstract. With the rise of AI-generated content (AIGC), phishing actors now possess richer linguistic capabilities and evasion techniques. Most existing detectors over-rely on mutable textual features, achieving high accuracy on clean data but degrading severely under text-focused adversarial manipulation. This mirrors the lab-to-real performance gap. We investigate invariant signals in phishing emails: even when attackers modify surface text, functional intent constrains relations among typed entities. Threat-actor tradecraft is described via high-level TTPs, but rule-based systems like Sigma express invariants only through manually curated, field-specific patterns, limiting flexibility. We introduce PhishSigma++, an entity-relation-based malicious email detector for RFC822 messages that generalizes Sigma's design. It extracts 40 typed entity classes, computes 5 cross-type relations to build a typed email graph, and uses particle swarm optimization (PSO) to select a sparse discriminative mask, supporting classification and type-level evidence summary. On 29,142 messages, PhishSigma++ achieves 0.9675 F1 on clean data and outperforms text-centric baselines under non-adaptive Good Word padding at \r{ho}=0.8. It maintains 0.9579 F1, while a token-based Bayesian filter collapses to 0.0243 and a DistilBERT phishing checkpoint falls to 0.7284. Compared with traditional Sigma rules, PhishSigma++ offers higher detection, broader relational invariance coverage, and data-driven feature selection. We also show that thresholded typed relation scores encode a useful fragment of Sigma-style field conditions, unifying hand-crafted rule logic and learned relation masks in a single-email framework.

</details>


### [177] [Every Bit, Everywhere, All at Once: A Binomial Multibit LLM Watermark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11653)
*Thibaud Gloaguen,Robin Staab,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二项式编码的多比特LLM水印新方法，通过在每个token位置直接编码载荷中的每个比特，并搭配有状态编码器动态调整编码压力，在64位载荷下实现了优于8种基线方法的准确率和鲁棒性，同时提出了每比特置信度评分作为更实用的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印技术在实际商业部署中需要支持多比特载荷（如用户ID、时间戳等）的编码，但现有方法在编码复杂payload时的性能不足，缺乏实用的评估指标。

Method: 提出二项式编码（binomial encoding），在每个token位置直接编码载荷的每个比特；搭配有状态编码器（stateful encoder），在生成过程中动态将编码压力重定向到未充分编码的比特上。

Result: 在多达64位载荷上与8种基线方法对比，所提方案在消息准确率和鲁棒性上均表现更优，且在大载荷和低失真场景下优势更为显著。

Conclusion: 所提方法在多比特水印任务上优于现有基线；现有评估指标缺乏实际洞察，每比特置信度评分是更实用的评估指标。

Abstract: With LLM watermarking already being deployed commercially, practical applications increasingly require multibit watermarks that encode more complex payloads, such as user IDs or timestamps, into the generated text. In this work, we propose a fundamentally new approach for multibit watermarking: introducing binomial encoding to directly encode every bit of the payload at every token position. We complement our approach with a stateful encoder that during generation dynamically redirects encoding pressure toward underencoded bits. Our evaluation against 8 baselines on up to 64-bit payloads shows that our scheme achieves superior message accuracy and robustness, with the gap to baseline methods widening in more relevant settings (i.e., large payloads and low-distortion regimes). At the same time, we challenge prior works' evaluation metrics, highlighting their lack of practical insights, and introduce per-bit confidence scoring as a practically relevant metric for evaluating multibit LLM watermarks.

</details>


### [178] [Behavioral Integrity Verification for AI Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11770)
*Yuhao Wu,Tung-Ling Li,Hongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出行为完整性验证（BIV）框架，通过声明能力与实际代码行为的类型化集合比较，发现OpenClaw注册表中80.0%的技能存在描述-实现偏差，并能在恶意技能检测上达到0.946的F1分数，优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体安全工作聚焦于检测恶意提示和运行时风险行为，但技能工件（skill artifact）本身的安全性未经校验。作者形式化定义了行为完整性验证（BIV）问题，旨在填补对技能声明能力与实际能力之间偏差进行系统性审计的空白。

Method: BIV框架通过确定性代码分析与LLM辅助的能力提取相结合，在共享分类体系下对声明能力和实际能力进行类型化集合比较，生成结构化证据，支持偏差分类、根因分类和恶意技能检测三类下游分析。

Result: 在49,943个OpenClaw技能上，发现80.0%的技能存在描述-实现偏差，其中81.1%源于开发疏忽，18.9%源于对抗意图，5.0%包含预测的多阶段攻击链。在906个恶意技能检测基准上，BIV的F1达0.946，超越基于规则和单次LLM推理的最先进基线。

Conclusion: 行为完整性审计能够大规模检测LLM智能体技能中的声明-实现偏差，揭示隐蔽威胁模式（如复合威胁类别），并为区分疏忽与恶意提供可操作的证据，表明该框架是保障智能体技能安全性的有效手段。

Abstract: Agent skills extend LLM agents with privileged third-party capabilities such as filesystem access, credentials, network calls, and shell execution. Existing safety work catches malicious prompts and risky runtime actions, but the skill artifact itself goes unverified. We formalize this as the behavioral integrity verification (BIV) problem: a typed set comparison between declared and actual capabilities over a shared taxonomy that bridges code, instructions, and metadata. The BIV framework instantiates this comparison by pairing deterministic code analysis with LLM-assisted capability extraction. The resulting structured evidence supports three downstream analyses: deviation taxonomy, root-cause classification, and malicious-skill detection. On 49,943 skills from the OpenClaw registry, the deviation taxonomy reveals a pervasive description-implementation gap: 80.0% of skills deviate from declared behavior, with four novel compound-threat categories surfaced. Root-cause classification finds that deviations are mostly oversight, not malice: 81.1% trace to developer oversight and 18.9% to adversarial intent, with 5.0% of skills carrying predicted multi-stage attack chains. On a 906-skill malicious-skill detection benchmark, BIV reaches an F1 of 0.946, outperforming state-of-the-art rule-based and single-pass LLM baselines. These results demonstrate behavioral integrity auditing for agent skills at scale.

</details>


### [179] [Five Attacks on x402 Agentic Payment Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11781)
*Zelin Li,Qin Wang,Zhipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对x402协议进行了系统性安全分析，发现其在授权、绑定、重放保护和Web层处理方面存在五个具体攻击面，并通过本地链、Base Sepolia和实时端点的可复现测试验证了这些攻击的可行性，同时审计了三个开源SDK和端点。所有攻击均可导致未付费服务或已付费被拒两种后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: x402协议旨在通过HTTP 402状态码实现Web原生微支付，但其结合同步HTTP授权与异步区块链结算的设计引入了传统Web和链上支付中不存在的跨层攻击面，亟需安全性分析。

Method: 结合形式化分析与经验性验证，提出五类具体攻击（授权、绑定、重放保护、Web层处理），在本地链、Base Sepolia测试网和实时端点上构建可复现测试环境进行验证，并审计三个开源SDK及对应端点。

Result: 所有五类攻击均被证实行之有效，可导致两种结果：未支付即获取服务（unpaid service），或已支付但被拒绝服务（paid-but-denied）。

Conclusion: x402协议在设计层面和实现层面均存在安全漏洞，需要引入针对性的缓解措施才能用于生产环境。

Abstract: The x402 protocol revives the HTTP 402 Payment Required status code to enable web-native micropayments across APIs, content, and agents. It combines synchronous HTTP authorization with asynchronous blockchain settlement and introduces a cross-layer attack surface absent from conventional web and on-chain payments. In this paper, we formally analyze x402 and empirically show that it is vulnerable in both design and implementation. We present five concrete attacks that reveal weaknesses in authorization, binding, replay protection, and web-layer handling, showing that x402 is vulnerable across multiple stages of the payment workflow. We validate these attacks through a reproducible testbed on local chains, Base Sepolia, and live endpoints and further audit three open-source SDKs and endpoints. Our results show that all five attacks are practical and can cause either unpaid service or paid-but-denied outcomes. We also propose practical mitigations.

</details>


### [180] [Proteus: A Self-Evolving Red Team for Agent Skill Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11891)
*Zhaojiacheng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Proteus框架，系统性地衡量LLM智能体技能在自适应攻击者面前的泄露风险。Proteus通过五轴攻击空间搜索、审计-沙箱-预言机评估流水线和跨轮变异，在8个初始单元上达到40-90%的ASR@5，并通过路径扩展和表面扩展生成438个同时绕过审计并产生危害的变体。最强审计器AI-Infra-Guard仍被突破41.3%的联合成功率，表明当前技能审核机制严重低估了自适应攻击的残余风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的第三方LLM智能体技能部署风险无法通过单次审计或提示级红队测试充分评估，因为真实的攻击者可以利用审计反馈和运行时反馈反复重写技能。需要一种方法衡量这种"自适应泄露"风险——即有预算的攻击者迭代修改技能直至通过审计并产生运行时危害。

Method: Proteus是一个灰盒自进化红队框架，主要包括：(1) 形式化的五轴技能攻击空间搜索；(2) 统一的审计-沙箱-预言机流水线，返回结构化审计结果和运行时证据指导跨轮变异；(3) 路径扩展（寻找已有攻击的替代实现）和表面扩展（将学习到的实现模式迁移到新的攻击目标）。

Result: 第一阶段8个单元中，Proteus在5轮内达到40-90%的攻击成功率(ASR@5)，且学习曲线斜率为正。第二阶段路径/表面扩展生成438个同时绕过审计且致命的变体；SkillVetter在每个单元的被绕过率≥93%，最强审计器AI-Infra-Guard的联合成功率仍达41.3%。

Conclusion: 当前技能审核机制在面对自适应、反馈驱动的攻击者时，显著低估了残余风险。Proteus框架有效揭示了这一漏洞，为未来更鲁棒的技能审核系统设计提供了关键洞察和评估基准。

Abstract: Agent skills extend LLM agents with reusable instructions, tool interfaces, and executable code, and users increasingly install third-party skills from marketplaces, repositories, and community channels. Because a skill exposes both executable behavior and context-setting documentation, its deployment risk cannot be measured by single-shot audits or prompt-level red teams alone: a realistic attacker can use audit and runtime feedback to repeatedly rewrite the skill. We frame this risk as \emph{adaptive leakage} -- whether a budgeted attacker can iteratively revise a skill until it passes audit and produces verified runtime harm -- and present \ours{}, a grey-box self-evolving red-team framework for measuring it. Proteus searches a formalized five-axis skill-attack space. Each candidate is evaluated through a unified audit-sandbox-oracle pipeline that returns structured audit findings and runtime evidence to guide cross-round mutation. Beyond initial evasion, Proteus performs path expansion, which finds alternative implementations of successful attacks, and surface expansion, which transfers learned implementation patterns to new attack objectives beyond the original seed catalogue. Across eight phase-1 cells, Proteus reaches 40--90\% Attack Success Rate at $5$ rounds (ASR@5) with positive learning-curve slopes on both evaluated auditors. Phase-2 path/surface expansion produces 438 jointly bypassing and lethal variants, with SkillVetter bypassed at $\geq 93\%$ in every cell and AI-Infra-Guard, the strongest public auditor we evaluate, still admitting up to 41.3\% joint-success. These results show that current skill vetting substantially underestimates residual risk when evaluated against adaptive, feedback-driven attackers.

</details>


### [181] [A microservices-based endpoint monitoring platform with predictive NLP models for real-time security and hate-speech risk alerting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11997)
*Darlan Noetzold,Anubis Graciela De Moraes Rossetto,Juan Francisco De Paz Santana,Valderi Reis Quietinho Leithard*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于微服务的统一平台，通过收集端点遥测数据并应用BERT等预测性NLP模型，实现实时安全与合规告警。该平台集成数据泄露防护、用户行为监控和仇恨言论检测等功能，实验显示文本分类准确率达87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解决方案（如生产力追踪、数据泄露防护、仇恨言论检测）相互孤立，缺乏跨信号关联能力，导致事件响应延迟。组织亟需一个统一平台来整合端点监控、安全分析和预测能力。

Method: 采用微服务架构，使用RabbitMQ进行事件摄入和路由，Redis实现低延迟数据访问和告警分发；基于Transformer模型（如BERT）进行文本分类，评估仇恨言论检测效果。
